# Session log

Append-only journal of working sessions — the context git history doesn't
record. A commit says *what* changed; an entry here says what the session set
out to do, what was decided and *why*, and where to resume.

## 2026-09-14T17:30Z — fix/214-model-selection-over-24-options — execute-phase (close-out reconciliation)
- **Commits:** 3 (`27137cde`, `050489f0`, `a5900c2e`)
- **Files:** `docs/fix/214-model-selection-over-24-options/{progress.md,review-findings.md}` + `CHANGELOG.md` + `CHANGELOG.es.md` + `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/package.json` + `README.md`/`README.es.md` — branch-level, 3 new commits on top of the audit-pr session
- **Summary:** Ran `execute-phase --fix 214` (unit loop mode, no explicit `P<n>`). Queue was empty: P1–P7 all ticked, each reconciled against repository evidence (commits present, validators green). Ran all preflight gates — dependency receipt v1 (closure none), pre-execution review `PLAN-REVIEW-PASS` (rp-214-20260914-001, digest `b3e777b7`), acceptance manifest `4ea24ed7` (frozen, F9 user-approved replan — tsc-first validators), phase-lint P1–P7 PASS (8/8 each). Gate `cd packages/pi-agentic-workflow && bun run test` → 214 pass / 0 fail; AC2–AC8 tsc-first validators 3/1/1/2/2/8/2/1 pass. Branch sat 1 commit ahead of origin (`2a3e2b69` — the plan-review receipt), which drove the workflow-status sensor into `NEEDS_INPUT` (crash-recovery AMBIGUOUS: empty queue + unpushed). Pushed the receipt, committed reconciliation `a5900c2e`, verified the plan receipt still `current` after the commit and tree clean. Sensor now reports fix-214 `CLEAN`.
- **Decisions:** (1) The queue was empty so this invocation was terminal close-out, not new implementation — the sensor AMBIGUOUS was the artifact, not a lost phase. (2) **Sensor false-positive for fix plan receipts:** `scripts/workflow-status.mjs:598` computes `boundParent = parent ?? receipt.parent`; a fix receipt carries `Parent SPEC snapshot: null`, which the parser returns as the literal string `"null"`, so the sensor calls `verify --parent null` → the verifier refuses (`invalid-value@/parentSpecSnapshotDigest`) → every done-with-open-PR fix unit is reported `plan-stage receipt is missing` (observed: fix-214, fix-191). The authoritative check without `--parent` returns `current: true`. Fix direction: treat literal `"null"` as absent for `unitKind: fix`. Recorded as a Proposal in progress.md (not `decisions.md` — creating that file adds a bound `decisions` snapshot row and voids the frozen plan receipt; empirically confirmed).
- **Next:** PR #217 open, awaiting human merge (`https://github.com/gtrabanco/agentic-workflow/pull/217`). After merge: `/plan-feature --next` for the next roadmap unit, or `/workflow-status` to discover startable work.

- **Summary:** Executed `execute-phase --fix 224` (the deterministic replan routing fix) end-to-end through all 8 phases. P1: implemented `scripts/unit-route.mjs` — a closed, deterministic route table (replan/decision/fold/execute/plan-from-issue, first-match), bounded read set, fail-closed exits (1 unknown/usage, 2 ambiguous, no route on failure), one sanitizer. Red-first test suite: 14 pass. P2: created `skills/replan-findings/` (new internal contract) with conditional progressive loading in both planners. P3: class-routed `next.suggested` into `workflow-status` sensor. P4–P5: converged one canonical replan destination across 8 routing skill surfaces and 3 tutorial pairs (EN+ES). P6: updated SKILLS.md, withdrew stale SENSOR_SIGNALS entry, bumped version manifest. P7: re-bundled Pi mirror (0.10.0). - **Decisions:** (1) The router lives in `scripts/unit-route.mjs` — a pure-MJS, deterministic script that reads only the bounded read set (review-findings.md, SPEC.md, ACCEPTANCE.md, each cited existing path) and outputs a fixed block (`unit, status, open-rows, route, next, rows, read-set, fingerprint`). No shell, byte-deterministic. (2) `replan-findings` is a new internal skill — loaded only when the router prints `route: replan` — so it never participates in the full planning preflight; it appends phases to the unit's own SPEC ledger under the hardening placement rule. (3) The `next.suggested` machine signal carries the unit identity; the `findings.fix_now[]` projection items are schema-frozen (`additionalProperties:false`), so the unit id cannot leak there. (4) Four route ceilings needed re-basing after the prose convergence: plan-feature:issue, plan-feature:scoped, plan-fix:issue, and four review-change routes — each raised to `ceil(measured × 1.10)` with a `sources` note naming fix #224. (5) The version pin in `scripts/review-loop-discipline.test.mjs:10j` is an exact string match on `version: 1.4.0`; the SPEC's mandatory bump of `fold-findings` to 1.5.0 requires advancing the pin with equal assertion strength. (6) A full `review-change` → `fold-findings` cycle ran after the PR open, finding and folding 12 findings (F1–F7, F13–F15, F19) across 3 commits, then re-bundling the Pi mirror to 0.10.1.

Verdict: **SPEC-REVIEW-PASS** (C1–C14 all pass, zero material open findings). One non-material info note F24 appended (sweep row 13's pointer gloss exceeds A:10's operative text — F18 precedent, no repair required). The review-spec skill completed the Product gate: the SPEC is now ready for `plan-feature` to plan the Engineering half. Notable: the §8 self-check caught a draft-receipt defect (fabricated 40-hex SHA expansion) before the report — verified green (`structural.fresh: true`, `current: true`, exit 0) after correction.

Turn contracts go at the top of each skill (not the bottom) specifically because weak models truncate/skim toward the end of a long document — this is the single highest-leverage fix from this session. PR close-out requires printing the URL in chat because "not every agent shows open PRs" (Hermes doesn't); a roadmap row of bare `done` is now treated as an unfinished unit, not a cosmetic gap. Detector skills got explicit, mechanical (grep/git log, not inference) checks for every hardening rule instead of trusting a model to notice drift — this is what actually makes the workflow self-healing across model strength.

- **Files:** `scripts/normative-drift.test.mjs`, `scripts/ledger-ownership.test.mjs`, `packages/agentic-workflow-schema/test/pre-execution-docs.test.mjs`, `packages/agentic-workflow-schema/test/release-contract.test.mjs`, `packages/agentic-workflow-schema/test/verification-docs.test.mjs`, `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/src/routing/catalogue.ts`, `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/test/skill-parity.test.mjs`
- **Summary:** Diagnosed 8 `bun test` failures from repo root and fixed 7 runner-compatibility bugs (bun vs node). Verified all three prescribed gates are green: `packages/agentic-workflow-schema` `bun run test` → 679/679, `packages/pi-agentic-workflow` `bun run test` → 140/140, `node --test scripts/...` → 34/34.
- **Decisions:** Four root causes identified: (1) `bun --test <file>` does NOT start the bun test runner — bun only starts a runner via the `test` subcommand, so passing `--test` as a flag under bun executes the file as a plain script (3 spawn sites: `normative-drift.test.mjs`, `ledger-ownership.test.mjs`, `release-contract.test.mjs`). Fix: runtime-detect `process.versions?.bun` and use `"test"` vs `"--test"`. (2) `verification-docs.test.mjs` resolved `tsc` via `process.cwd()` → fails from repo root; fix: anchor to `import.meta.url`. (3) `readdirSync` order differs between bun and node for the same directory — affected `readCatalogue` (alias-coverage AC3) and `skill-parity.test.mjs` AC2 fixture. Fix: sorted enumeration in production + order-independent assertions in tests. (4) `console.log` object formatting differs — bun multi-line double-quoted, node single-line single-quoted; affected `pre-execution-docs.test.mjs` AC8 runtime assertion. Fix: regex tolerant to whitespace/quote variation.
- **Next:** 7 files uncommitted. Commit them as `test(...)` or `fix(...)` per repo convention. Additionally: route-budget breach (issue #176) is pre-existing at HEAD — deferred until all feature/fix batch settles; no action needed now.

- **Summary:** Drafted and committed the fix SPEC for issue #179 (declared-ledger-delta-receipts), building on the plan repair batch (4d5e1dd5 + 25a0764c) from the prior session. The fix makes execution-ledger bookkeeping (SPEC task tick flips, obligation status cells) a declared amendment class: the sensor answers `declared-delta` instead of voiding plan receipts as `stale-source-revision`, `audit-pr` treats the answer as a non-blocking warning, and `verify`-axis staleness findings get a foldable `fix-now` route.
- **Decisions:** (D1) `declared-delta` is a `reasonCode` (not a top-level status or new exit code) — consumers already key on it and fail-closed defaults survive. (D2) Class check is total-diff-per-path + commit-coverage, not per-commit byte matching (rebase-fragile). (D3) Uncommitted ticks fail closed via `stale-artifact-content`. (D4) `ACCEPTANCE.md` excluded (blob-bound). (D5) Retro declarations are fact records by fold-findings, not reclassification. (D6) `execute-phase`'s gate and `workflow-status` 6a stay fail-closed (out of scope). (D7) Pure comparator never emits the code (no git/ledger access); documented refinement. (D8) Dependencies on #170-#172 recorded — dependency gate blocks execution until they merge.
- **Next:** /review-plan fix-179 (independent context reviews the plan before any phase runs). After review PASS: /execute-phase --fix 179 (execution gated by dependency on features 30/31/32 merging). Also: fix-162 F12 fold-closure can be done after this merges (retro declarations + `folded: yes` on fix-162's own fold loop).

D30-5 escalation: width (changed file outside cited union) or size (>200 lines or >15 files). D30-6 cap counts delta cycles; third cycle user-only. D30-7 reproducer handoff: recheck cell materialized, never re-derived. NRS contradiction recorded (snapshot 2026-08-30 stale on rows 28–34; routed to `resolve-repository-state`). ## 2026-09-09T00:20:00Z — fix/154-settings-picker-search-bulk-chain — manual
- **Commits:** 12 (`18ba3c3b`…`3a7e7042`) → PR [#203](https://github.com/gtrabanco/agentic-workflow/pull/203) OPEN, awaiting the human merge
- **Files:** `docs/fix/154-settings-picker-search-bulk-chain/{SPEC.md,ACCEPTANCE.md,progress.md}`, `docs/fix/README.md`, `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/{README.md,README.es.md,CHANGELOG.md,CHANGELOG.es.md,package.json,bun.lock}`, `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/src/{routing/types,routing/dispatch,extension/factory,extension/index,settings/console,settings/view,settings/picker}.ts`, `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/test/{alias-coverage,config-merge,picker-filter,settings-console,unavailable-stop}.mjs`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `CHANGELOG.es.md`
- **Summary:** Executed all 9 phases (P1–P9) of fix #154 on branch `fix/154-settings-picker-search-bulk-chain` — the settings-console picker defects A–D (no filter, no scroll, no current value, one command per pass) plus the ordered model fallback chain E. P1 extended `ModelSetting` to accept 1–4 references as an ordered chain with schema validation and merge preservation. P2 rewrote dispatch to probe a chain via `ctx.find` + `ctx.hasConfiguredAuth` only (no session mutation, `setModel` ≤ 1/turn), applying the first usable entry and on exhaustion naming every candidate and skip reason. P3 registered `/aw-settings` as a second command. P4 added a searchable windowed picker: `SettingsUi.pick` seam + `@earendil-works/pi-tui@0.85.1` pin + `src/settings/picker.ts` with an in-package subsequence/slash-aware filter driving a client-side `SelectList` rebuild, wired via `ctx.ui.custom()` in TUI and `select` fallback in non-TUI. P5 added field-choice editing, current-value preselection, byte-identical no-change save, and an ordered chain builder in the model field. P6 implemented one-pass bulk apply/clear over several commands with per-command advisory warnings. P7 updated both READMEs (chain schema, picker behaviour, alias), bumped version 0.7.2 → 0.8.0 (minor), added bilingual changelog rows. P8 hardening & close-out: full gate re-run (171 pass/0 fail), docs-check clean, fix-index flipped to `done`, PR #203 opened with `Closes #154` via `--body-file`. A post-open review-findings fold (F2, decision-required on chain-edit visibility) yielded P9: amendment A1 to the SPEC + chain-builder seeded from value in force + `0c319fd8` commit. Gate green throughout (171 pass/0 fail, tsc + full suite, AC15 README checks green).
- **Decisions:** (1) Chain-probe dispatch uses only `ctx.find`+`hasConfiguredAuth` (no `setModel` during probing) because mutating session state mid-dispatch would break AC3's "setModel ≤ once per turn" contract — the probe is read-only, and `setModel` fires exactly once on the first successful pick. (2) In-package subsequence/slash-aware filter (`filterReferences`) rather than relying on pi-tui's native prefix filter, because the SPEC's AC2 requires subsequence matching and the in-package filter must drive the SelectList rebuild per keystroke. (3) Field-choice editing (ask only the marked field, keep unmarked at merged value) so a no-change save is byte-identical — this is a contract requirement (AC4) and a UX guarantee (user shouldn't be asked for a value they don't want to change). (4) Bulk apply applies one field pass to every selected command (model via chain builder, thinking plain) and warns per command when a reference is absent from the live registry — warnings are advisory, never blocking the write. (5) `/aw-settings` alias must NOT go in the README command table (AC15 pins the table to the skill catalogue) — it's documented in the settings-console section only. (6) Fix-index row was flipped to `done` before the PR was opened, then updated to `done · [#203]` after — the two-step convention (pre-open `done` for "built", post-open `done · [#]` for "with PR link").
- **Next:** PR #203 is open (forge-verified via `gh pr view 203` → MERGEABLE, state OPEN) — the mandatory end review `/review-change` on the changed HEAD, then `/audit-pr` as the merge gate. After merge: human merges PR #203.

## 2026-09-09T00:30Z — main — manual
- **Commits:** 30 (`aff803dd…f48dff00`)
- **Files:** 51 (skills references, package.jsons, CLAUDE.md, CHANGELOG, workflows, test pins, budgets, scripts usage strings, lockfiles)
- **Summary:** Long design-and-implement session. Started with a user request to make review-change more deterministic and cheaper (like issues #183–#186). Through investigation and iterative discussion, landed on three interrelated decisions that produced 7 feature/issue proposals and one large PR. (1) Binary proposal (#196) was proposed, measured (78 MB/target on Bun 1.4.2), then declined in favor of a JS package with node/bun as declared requirement — the installation method (npx/bunx/pnpx) already implies the runtime, so binary escape-hatch machinery was disproportionate. (2) PR #199 (`feat/197-bun-first-runtime`) delivered the bun-first runtime migration: package scripts invoke bun as the actual runtime (not just installer) with explicit `test:node` fallback variants, the convention is declared once in CLAUDE.md (bun → node fallback → `AGENTIC_WORKFLOW_RUNTIME` override → `npm_config_user_agent` hint; node shebangs stay portable), both publish workflows gain `node-compat` CI jobs enforcing the fallback, and dependencies were pinned to exact versions. Both PRs (#193 and #199) merged in this session. (3) Issues #194–#196, #198, #200, #201 created/updated; #195 split into #201 (pass-model routing) + alias folded into #154 (settings surface).
- **Decisions:** (a) Node shebangs stay `#!/usr/bin/env node` — a shebang cannot express "bun else node", and direct `./x.mjs` execution is not a documented path; bun-first lives in every documented invocation. (b) `docs/features/*` frozen artifacts (SPECs, progress, testing, receipts) are never bulk-rewritten — they record what was run, not instructions. (c) `peerDependencies` stays as range by nature (resolves against the host's pi version); only `dependencies`/`devDependencies` are exact. (d) The `check-skill-context.test.mjs` "over-budget reference" failure pre-exists on main under node (verified via stash) — triaged as #200, not weakened. (e) Both package bumps (4.1.1 / 0.7.2) carry both the bun-first change and the dependency pinning — single patch release, no intermediate version. (f) `resolve-repository-state` was not invoked for the frozen receipts (REPOSITORY_STATE.md F002–F005) — historical facts unchanged; the CLAUDE.md runtime convention addition is the only doc surface that changed.
- **Next:** PR #193 and #199 are both MERGED. Next steps: (1) `review-change` on merged main for the bun-first surface (#194 orchestration issue and #196 producer crate remain open for new work). (2) `plan-feature --from-issue 196` when ready to build the `packages/agentic-workflow` producer crate. (3) `plan-feature --from-issue 198` for the per-skill package layout + shared runtime resolver. (4) Triage #200 (stale over-budget fixture) in any cycle touching `scripts/`.

- **Summary:** Investigated ponytail (DietrichGebert/ponytail) and its "lazy senior dev" decision ladder (YAGNI → codebase reuse → stdlib → native platform → installed dep → one line → minimum). Benchmarks: −54% LOC, −20% cost, −27% time on real agent sessions, 100% safety. Key insight: the ladder runs AFTER understanding the problem, not instead of it — and never cuts validation, error handling, security, or accessibility. This addresses the root cause of slow but solid skills: design-feature and plan-feature don't answer reuse questions, producing under-researched SPECs that generate review-loop findings (#205). Folded the reuse-first concept into #206's convergence pass (not a separate skill/issue). Registered #206 and #207 in ROADMAP.md as features 46 (`design-implementation-research`) and 47 (`reuse-first-design`). Closed duplicate release-policy issues #208 and #210, keeping #209 as the canonical version policy.
- **Decisions:** (1) Ponytail's ladder adapted, not copied verbatim — no ultra-lazy mode (our phase-based contracts and gates stay). (2) "Build or reuse" determination integrated into #206's convergence pass, not a standalone design extension. (3) Library quality bar: actively maintained OR abandoned-but-stable (frozen, complete, no known bugs, no realistic bug surface); never app core/framework; never micro-packages. (4) Three reuse profiles (Standalone/Cautious/Manual) live in init-workspace preferences, not permanent route text (avoids budget inflation until #176 slimming). (5) All releases minor (not major) until #176 is merged — feature 38's version bump must respect this.

## 2026-09-11T00:30Z — feat/38-workflow-status-sensor-script — manual (diagnostic)
- **Commits:** 0 (diagnostic session, no repo changes)
- **Files:** GitHub issue created at https://github.com/gtrabanco/pi-nan-provider/issues/2
- **Summary:** Diagnosed the intermittent `"Model response failed: Stream ended without finish_reason"` error reported by the user when using GLM5.3-flash via the NaN provider (`api.nan.builders`). Confirmed it is an **upstream remote flake**: the LiteLLM gateway intermittently cuts the SSE stream before emitting the final chunk with `finish_reason`. Verified by (a) tracing through `pi-ai`'s `openai-completions.js:501` where the error is thrown, (b) replaying ~40 live streaming requests (plain text, tool calls, high reasoning, `include_usage:true`, 4096–8192 max_tokens) — all succeeded but gateway also returned 429s, confirming instability. No deterministic reproduction.
- **Decisions:** (1) Root fix: add `"supportsFinishReason": false` to `NAN_COMPAT` in `scripts/generate-models.ts` (~line 79) and regenerate catalog, so pi-ai treats cut streams as `stop`/`toolUse` instead of throwing. (2) Better fix: add retry for this specific error in `pi-ai`'s streaming pipeline (current `retryProviderRequest` only handles HTTP errors). (3) Root cause: NaN's gateway should always emit `finish_reason` + `[DONE]` or an error object. (4) Created GitHub issue #2 on pi-nan-provider with full diagnosis and fix proposal.

## 2026-09-10TXX:XXZ — fix/on-settle-keep-routed-model — manual (mini fix: keep routed model after settle)
- **Commits:** 2 (`8ee6d33a`, `ed4b02b0`)
- **Files:** `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/src/{config/{defaults,merge,schema,types}.ts,dispatch.ts,settings/{console,view}.ts}`, `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/test/{helpers/session,config-merge,default-inherit,on-settle-keep,restore-after-settle,settings-console,shipped-adapter}.test.mjs`, `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/scripts/mutation-check.mjs`, `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/README.md` + `README.es.md`, `CHANGELOG.md` + `CHANGELOG.es.md`, `docs/features/27-pi-agentic-workflow/SPEC.md`, `packages/pi-agentic-workflow/package.json` (21 files, +396/−63)
- **Summary:** Implemented the `onSettle` config key for `@gtrabanco/pi-agentic-workflow` (0.8.0 → 0.9.0). Added `onSettle: "keep" | "restore"`, shipped default `"keep"`. After a routed command settles (`agent_settled`), the model and thinking level the command ran on now **stay** in the open chat window instead of being restored to the pre-dispatch snapshot. The historical restore contract (AC8) is now the explicit `onSettle: "restore"` opt-in. A follow-up edit or question keeps running on the model that planned it; the operator switches manually (via `/model`/Ctrl+P) when they want something cheaper. Opened PR #211.
- **Decisions:** (1) Default is `"keep"` (not `"restore"`): the owner explicitly wants the model to persist after a routed command so they can continue working with it without re-selecting. (2) `undoInFlight` and dispatch-failure rollback **always** restore, regardless of policy — they are not settles (a failed send means the turn never started; a console release is the operator explicitly backing out). (3) The operator's own mid-turn `/model`/Ctrl+P choice is still never overwritten under either policy. (4) `onSettle` is a single root config key — per-command override deliberately out of scope for this mini fix. (5) No skill re-bundle — only the router behaviour changed. (6) SPEC 27 AC8 enmended to record the changed contract (frozen fact updated via amendment note referencing commit `8ee6d33a`).
- **Next:** /audit-pr on PR #211 (`https://github.com/gtrabanco/agentic-workflow/pull/211`) for the merge gate.

Seven isolated passes: code+usage-docs, security, verify, perf, brand, folded-row re-verification sweep (31/31 rows repaired), plus isolated classifier and debt transform. Found 7 fix-now rows (F44–F50), 2 decision-required (D-1 PR composition, D-2 language policy), 2 proposals (P-a box-8 vacuous vocabulary, P-b perf gate 4th recurrence). The fold-findings session then folded F44-F50 in two atomic commits (4e206499: script fixes + corpus; ad55802d: record fidelity), reconciled the settled replan flags for F28-F33 (23d663da), and continued through subsequent review→fold cycles F51-F90 across additional sessions. Heading/task regexes untouched. (2) F45 fix: widened box-8 to accept `exits N` and `exit code N` — the SPEC freezes "a backticked command and an expected outcome" without enumerating forms, so the corpus is the contract. (4) F49 fix: verdict-like literals (`Phase-lint:`, `verdict`, `fingerprint`) broken up inside `sanitizeEcho` with a regex insert, rule decisions still read the raw title. ## 2026-09-13T09:00Z — feat/37-phase-lint-script — manual (execute-phase 37 P6 + P7 close-out)
- **Commits:** 2 (`d77f1f48`, `36cfeb8e`)
- **Files:** `scripts/phase-lint.mjs` (+52/−14), `scripts/phase-lint.test.mjs` (+166, 6 new fixtures), `docs/features/37-phase-lint-script/{TASKS.md,testing.md,decisions.md,progress.md,known-issues.md}`, `docs/features/ROADMAP.md`
- **Summary:** Executed the remaining phases of feature 37 (`/execute-phase 37`) on the `37-plan-7` re-cut plan: P6 (grammar conformance — box-5 standalone `or` detection + fence-aware parser) and P7 (close-out re-run — full gate, dev scenarios, roadmap flip, PR body refresh). The branch was already at HEAD `bf500374` from the earlier replan-7; PR #212 was OPEN.
- **Decisions:** (1) Box-5 standalone-`or` uses a Unicode lookaround `/(?<!\p{L}\p{N}_-)or(?!\p{L}\p{N}_-))/iu` — no word char and no hyphen adjacent on either side, so `editor` and `equal-or-greater` stay one token. The retired `hasEitherOr` helper is removed (the old shape is subsumed). The shipped finding message `offers either/or alternatives` is retained so the immutable decision-scan corpus assertion holds. (2) Fence parsing: opening = 3+ backticks (optional info string) or tildes; closing = only the same char at equal-or-greater length; every line inside inert; unclosed → EOF. The phase header registers fences before any other grammar rule. (3) The `PLAN-REVIEW-37-8` receipt became stale by design after P6 landed (changedPaths TASKS/decisions/testing); only a fresh `/review-plan` restores it — never refresh. (4) `gh pr edit` hit a GraphQL "Projects classic" deprecation error, so the PR body was refreshed via the REST API (`gh api --method PATCH ...`). (5) The unrelated untracked `docs/fix/214-model-selection-over-24-options/` tree collides with `git status --porcelain -- docs/`; it was disclosed in known-issues.md and progress.md, not committed or removed.
- **Next:** `/review-change` on HEAD `36cfeb8e` (mandatory end review). Fold only on REVIEW-FAIL, then re-run. Merge gate `/audit-pr 212`. After merge: /plan-feature --next.

The fix replaces three contradictory prose routing destinations with one deterministic router script (`unit-route.mjs`), a conditional replan entry contract (`replan-findings` skill), class-routed `next.suggested` emission in the status sensor, and converges all documentation surfaces on the canonical router command.
- **Decisions:** (1) Router kept as a separate script from `workflow-status.mjs` rather than extending it (Decision 1 from SPEC), so the route decision is not paid for on every sensor invocation and a mutable routing table stays out of the envelope consumer's back. (2) The router prints `route:` / `fingerprint:` machine lines with exit code 0 (route determined), 1 (usage/unknown), 2 (ambiguous) — mirroring the linter's pattern, avoiding route-encoding in exit codes. (3) The bounded read set is printed (not performed) — the router lists paths for the planner to read, keeping the router read-only. (4) AC5's dogfood leg was fixture-based (unit 37's ledger at `e1e282c5` copied into `scripts/fixtures/unit-route/`) because the target branch was unmerged at the bound revision; the live invocation was recorded as informational post-merge. (5) A second convergence cycle (cycle 2) was entered after the first repair batch — RP-224-1…5 all genuinely resolved, but a new finding (RP-224-6: review-change version-bump gap) appeared, which the cycle 3 batch also repaired. CONVERGENCE-ANOMALY block was logged at the time.
- **Next:** fix-224 is fully executed — PR is open, merge is pending the human merge. The `review-change` and `fold-findings` findings (F1–F19) are the most recent fold batch and should be committed. After the human merges the PR, the fix-index row flips to `done · [#<pr>](<pr-url>)` and the repo gate (`node --test scripts/*.test.mjs`) should be re-run for a clean green baseline.

- **Summary:** Completed review-change cycle 14 on unit 37-phase-lint-script (PR #212): all 85 folded rows re-verified (83 repaired, 2 regressions → F100/F101/F102/F104/F105/F111), persisted 13 new fix-now rows F100–F112, folded 12 of them in 3 atomic batches (gate 383/383 exit 0). Diagnosed and filed the replan-routing dead-end (issue #224): four independent surfaces grew around one missing owner so a replan finding could never reach the planner. Designed and froze a fix plan: `scripts/unit-route.mjs` as a deterministic router with bounded read set, `skills/replan-findings/` as a conditional internal contract, class-routed `next.suggested` emission, and four contradictory prose destinations converging on one canonical target. The worktree was then picked up by another session which executed all 7 phases, folded 4 of 13 review findings, and opened PR #225 (OPEN, mergeStateStatus CLEAN, awaiting fold-findings completion + human merge).
- **Decisions:** (1) **Router as a new script, not an extension of workflow-status.** The sensor answers "what is the state"; the router answers "which command does this unit take now". Merging them would make every sensor invocation pay for a route decision. (2) **Load decision is deterministic code, not prose.** The user directive was explicit: the load decision, the route decision and the selection of the finding are taken deterministically by code. The router prints `route: replan|decision|fold|execute|plan-from-issue` and the internal contract loads only on `replan`. (3) **Bounded read set, not full preflight.** The replan path reads only the selected ledger rows + their verification marks + cited files + SPEC/ACCEPTANCE; the full `planning-preflight` read is skipped. (4) **Phase-lint mechanics for root non-Markdown files.** Probed: `skills.sh.json`/`model-routing.yml` answer `verdict BLOCKED: unparseable` under any layer because the box-2 prefix table has no root-file entry — plans cannot name these files; routed out of scope to unit 37's linter. (5) **Unmerged linter used for phase linting.** `scripts/phase-lint.mjs` doesn't exist on `main` (ships with unit 37); phases were linted with the 37-branch linter and the disclosure recorded in the SPEC. (6) **`next.suggested` signal was documented but never emitted** (`SENSOR_SIGNALS.md:74-76` promised it; the schema declared it; the code never wrote it). The fix implements it routed by class. (7) **readiness box 5 conflict resolved.** `evidence-grounding` says M/L features use `planning-evidence.md`; the fix contract says "no separate planning artifacts".   **A. Token audit (measured before cutting):** 39 `SKILL.md` files (35.5K words) + 87 `references/*.md` (59.5K words) = ~127K tokens instruction surface. Turn tax: 11-box contract + `→ Next:` + envelope re-emitted per turn. Review loops: up to 12–16 cycles (unit 37: 12 cycles, F1–F90).

  **B. Research (papers verifiable on arXiv):** Chain of Draft (2502.18600) — CoT accuracy at 7.6% tokens; TALE (2412.18547) — token budgets compress reasoning with slight loss; Stop Overthinking (2503.16419) / Do NOT Think That Much (2412.21187) — overthinking measurable and quality-neutral; LLMs Cannot Self-Correct (2310.01798, ICLR'24) — intrinsic self-correction without new evidence fails (validates non-convergent loops); Let Me Speak Freely (2408.02442) — format restrictions degrade reasoning (structure machine-consumed data only); SWE-agent (2405.15793) — interface design drives performance; Lost in the Middle (2307.03172) — attention budget degrades with volume. Primary sources: Anthropic Agent Skills (progressive disclosure 3 levels), Cognition (single-threaded linear agent + compression model), Claude Code best practices.

  **C. 8 new GitHub issues created:** #226 (machine-checked turn contract), #227 (deterministic artifact emitters), #228 (retire progress.md), #229 (two-level SPEC), #230 (executable golden fixture), #231 (design-interview batching), #232 (per-release bilingual), #233 (remove ship-roadmap).

  **D. Issue merges (owner-approved consolidation):** #228 absorbed into #229 (Phase 2 satellite-file retirement — same surfaces, one unit instead of two); #152 absorbed into #232 (Phase 1 drift checker — same surface). #183 partially re-scoped (fixture metric row → #230; anti-flakiness + property/fuzz stay).

  **E. Comment additions:** #194 (review-pack 10→1 consolidation rides the deterministic-review-change unit); #205 (one-line findings format + evidence rule from paper); #182 (CLI computes evidence freshness columns); #173 (order note — adopts 52's machine profile); #176 (D18 scope: plan-feature family 3→1 consolidation).

  **F. Roadmap update PR #234 (docs/roadmap-bureaucracy-order → main):** New rows 52–58 with issue references, row 34 folded → 57, new section "Bureaucracy-reduction execution order (2026-09-15)". Execution order: Phase 0 quick-wins (fix #224, 52, 56, 55, 48) → Phase 1 loop policy (~40–60% lever: 31→32→35→42→51→50) → Phase 2 artifact formats (~60% authoring: 44→54→53→40) → Phase 3 structural (57→58→33) → #176 always last with D18.

  **G. Key design decision:** Principle = "eliminate narration, not checks." Scripts run and paste, model only judges materiality. Structure data consumed by machines, prose only where the model reasons. Estimated ~60–70% process-token reduction by #176 with quality guarantees intact.

- **Summary:** Feature 40 (`versioned-skills-releases`, issue #180) design — interviewed on tool adoption decision (changesets file-format vs tool), unified version train across skills + pi package + schema package, channel model (canary auto, stable/manual, alpha→beta→rc→stable promotion cycle), Bun-style version shape for canary (`<base>-canary.<date>.<n>`), force-version override for marketing/release needs, channel auto-reset to alpha after stable release. Verified PR #225 is still OPEN (not merged yet, so the first train version cannot be fixed). Determined that feature 40 has **no remaining hard dependency** — 30/37/38 merged, 31/32 have no surface overlap. English-only policy confirmed (`.es.md` siblings deleted, bilingual rule retired until feature 57). Design not yet written to SPEC — the interview loop was interrupted by the english-only cleanup rebase.
- **Decisions:** (1) **Changesets file-format, not the tool.** Zero cross-deps between the three packages makes changesets' workspace machinery unnecessary; the file format captures the PR-time intent without the workspace root. (2) **Unified train version.** Skills + pi package + schema share one release number; per-skill `version:` stays as change ledger. (3) **Channel ladder auto-resets.** Stable release → next release auto-promotes to alpha (unstable). Canary = next-stable-as-current-subchannel. (4) **No fixed version number in design.** The first train version is computed at launch from accumulated changesets, not predicted (PR #225 may land before this feature, affecting the count).
- **Next:** Write the SPEC Product half for feature 40, then `/review-spec 40-versioned-skills-releases`. Also push/PR the english-only cleanup (`docs/english-only-interim` → main).

- **Summary:** User-initiated cycle 3 of review-change (adversarial N=2) on PR #236, starting from reviewed head `126ef68e` (F5–F7 fold batch tip). R1 (correctness) raised 6 candidates, R2 (security) 8; isolated verifier confirmed 9, refuted 1 (detached-HEAD: both engines print `fail box3: no-commits`, not `ok`). Two major, two floor-reclassed-to-med findings surfaced — all against the same two call sites: box5's `git status` read and box4's `gh pr view` query. Classified via isolated `review-implementation` (4 fix-now med, 5 ignore lows) and `review-debt` (5 lows became trigger-carrying debt notes). All four fix-now findings folded in one atomic batch: the status read is now pinned with `--untracked-files=all` (overrides repo-local `status.showUntrackedFiles=no` which could hide untracked files) and `--no-optional-locks` (prevents the read from rewriting `.git/index`, contradicting the read-only floor); the PR query switched from `gh pr view <branch>` to `gh pr list --head <branch> --state open` (a missing PR was previously indistinguishable from a gh outage, and an all-numeric branch name like `123` was read as a PR number). The `gh` PATH-stub gained per-verb answers (`pr list` vs `pr view`, independent view exit) because real gh exits nonzero for a branch with no PR — that error is the whole of F9. Decisions.md gained ED-52-10 (box4 query) and ED-52-11 (box5 pinning); SPEC box4 and gh-drift risk rows updated. Gate green at head: shim 31 cases, engine+parity 56/56, grammar 52/52, normative-drift 17/17, sensor 56/56, context 40 skills, pi skill-parity 7/7. No acceptance blob changed (`c088a621…`). PR subsequently merged to main (merge commit `251383f5`, merged 2026-09-17T00:14:15+02:00).
- **Decisions:** (1) F9's fix chose `gh pr list --head` over alternatives (separate `gh api` call, shim-level awk parsing) because `pr list` is the idiomatic gh command for "show me this branch's PRs" and answers the unobservable case (empty array, exit 0) without the indirection that made `pr view` exit nonzero for both "no PR" and "network error". (2) The index-bytes assertion (F10) is structurally stronger than a porcelain before/after comparison — the assertion goes red against the unchanged engine, proving the existing `read-only` test structurally cannot observe an index write. (3) Five debt triggers each carry their own re-trigger condition and are not blocking: stale origin/HEAD → wrong-box cause, `--nope --help` wording, box1 self-disable on trunk repos, engine profile trust-model caveat, usage `turn-contract` without a `bin` entry.

The batch resolved two causes: (1) a grep alternation neutered by markdown-table cell-escaping (F1), and (2) owned criteria/invariants whose carrying row or task never landed (F2, F5) or landed only as prose without a task entry (F3, F4). - **Decisions:** (1) **F1 root cause: escaped-pipe grep cannot fail.** The `\|` alternation in ACCEPTANCE.md/O1 was backslash-escaped to survive the markdown cell — GNU grep -E reads it as a literal string. Convention established: use repeated `-e` patterns (table-safe, copy-paste executable, same semantics). (2) **Phase-lint box 4 counts `→` in task bullets as step chains.** Fixed by stating outcomes as prose (`exits 0`, not `→ exit 0`) in task checkboxes. Done-when lines are exempt. (3) **Phase fingerprints cover shape, not content.** Adding a task rotates `P<n>:<layer>:<n-tasks>:<title>` — must re-sync the SPEC's recorded fingerprint. (4) **Concurrent modification detected.** Another actor modified `ROADMAP.md` (folded 48+55 → new 59) mid-turn; only this unit's files were committed. (5) **F6 stays open.** Product-class info finding — `design-feature`'s resolver; editing the Product half now would rotate the parent snapshot the plan receipt binds.

## 2026-09-17T12:00Z — feat/55-executable-golden-fixture — plan repair (PF-55-01..04)
- **Commits:** 2 (`2ecc4915`, `0e076113`)
- **Files:** `docs/features/55-executable-golden-fixture/{SPEC.md, ACCEPTANCE.md, planning-findings.md, progress.md}`, `docs/features/ROADMAP.md`
- **Summary:** Repaired the plan after PLAN-REVIEW-FAIL (PF-55-01…04) with an in-place plan revision, not appended phases. The four findings were: (PF-55-01) ACCEPTANCE AC5's slug-grep was malformed — a markdown table cell cannot encode a regex needing both a literal pipe and an alternation (both must be written `\|`, rendering identically), so the rendered form exempted the whole doc (vacuous pass) while the raw form exempted nothing (false fail). Fixed to SPEC AC5's semantics using a bracketed literal pipe `[|]` plus one alternation; the rendered table-cell command is byte-identical to the new `## Commands` entry, returns exactly the 3 embedded-block lines pre-slim (non-vacuous) and empty post-slim. (PF-55-02) Doc-slim arithmetic wrong — replaced the incorrect "173 embedded + 54 prose" claim with the measured ledger: moves 205 lines (72+14+65+54) + run-log re-join 11 blank separators = 167; condense 18 prose lines + 1-line log intro = 148. Added evidence row PE-017 with the frozen probe. (PF-55-03) O2 mis-phased/duplicated O7 — re-scoped O2 to the P1-runnable file-fact (audit-target tree committed at five paths, validator `git ls-files`); O7 keeps the P3 suite assertion with distinct evidence. (PF-55-04) O12 lifecycle wrong — now states the full status machine (`planned` → `in-progress` → `done · [PR]`); ROADMAP row 55's stale `next: /review-spec 55` tail replaced with the passed spec receipt + `/review-plan 55`. Acceptance receipt blob re-frozen (`2b832ac9…`). All four planning-findings rows flipped `resolved`.
- **Decisions:** (1) **In-place plan revision over appended phases** — the findings were all about frozen plan text being wrong (a malformed gate, bad arithmetic, mis-phased obligations, stale lifecycle prose), not about missing work. Appending repair phases would have created phases that re-state already-present content. (2) **Markdown pipe limitation is structural** — a regex alternation `\|` and a literal pipe `\|` look identical in rendered markdown, so the ACCEPTANCE table cell MUST use a different encoding (bracket class `[|]`) and point to the byte-exact command in `## Commands` for execution. (3) **`scripts/unit-route.mjs` reads only `review-findings.md`**, so plan-stage findings in `planning-findings.md` are invisible to the router — it printed `open-rows: 0 · route: execute` for a unit whose plan review FAILED. The planner must act directly on the failed receipt. (4) **`spec-product-v1` excludes the Engineering half**, so the product receipt stayed fresh (digest `d8f9c47f…` unchanged) while the shared `SPEC.md` file's derived revision moved — `verify --stage spec` still prints `stale-source-revision` but the product projection is byte-identical. (5) The plan snapshot binds SPEC+ACCEPTANCE+decisions but NOT planning-findings.md/progress.md, so the resolving ledger commit did not rotate `artifactRevisionId`; only the repair commit did.
- **Next:** `/review-plan 55-executable-golden-fixture` — the repaired plan needs an independent review before any phase is executed. The review returned PLAN-REVIEW-PASS (plan-review-55-20260917-5, re-derived parent), enabling P1–P4 execution (logged in the execute-phase entry). PR #240 is OPEN, awaiting human merge.

## 2026-09-19T00:00Z — feat/31-planning-review-materiality — review-change + fold-findings (cycle 3)

**Branch:** `feat/31-planning-review-materiality` · **Review head:** 6e485a4d (pre-fold) → **HEAD:** 6e485a4d (after fold commits) · **PR:** #243 (OPEN, CLEAN, mergeStateStatus=CLEAN) · **Cycle:** 3 (2 REVIEW-RAN marks from fold) · **Clean tree at close:** yes · **Pushed:** origin ✓

**Summary:** Ran /review-change cycle 3 (delta→full escalation: fold commits added `scripts/pre-execution-sensor.test.mjs` outside cited locations). All four isolation passes ran (code, security, verify, perf). Acceptance verified green at reviewed head: root suite 509/509 exit 0, schema 717/0 exit 0, gate:pre-execution 14/14 exit 0, pi mirror 214/0 exit 0, route budgets PASS 22, phase-lint 5×PASS, all AC greps hold. /review-change reported REVIEW-FAIL with two new findings: **F6** (high, verify) — root suite red because six route ceilings grew past their declared values; **F7** (med, code) — `determinationLine` parser (the F2 fold) dropped the line anchor, so a determination missing `- Artifact revision:` borrows the next block's value and E6's fail-closed check answers fresh. Fold-findings processed F3, F6, F7 (all three `folded: no`). All 3 folded, gates green at HEAD 6e485a4d, tree clean.

**Decisions:**
- **F7 root cause and fix:** The fold fix for F2 replaced the original `determinationLine` regex (`new RegExp(\`^[ \\\\t]*${label}:[ \\\\t]*(.+)$\`, "m")`) with an anchorless version (`new RegExp(\`${label}:[ \\\\t]*([^\\\\n\u00b7]+)\`)`) to support the E5 ` · `-separated line grammar. The anchor removal is load-bearing: a label now matches anywhere in the chunk, including a later block (a `## Pre-execution review receipt v1 — plan` block that also carries `- Artifact revision:`). Reproducer: determination (no revision line) followed by a plan receipt block → `revision: "ZZRECEIPT"` instead of `null`; E6's fail-closed identity check silently answers `fresh: true`. Fix: bound the field match to the determination block's own field lines by stopping the chunk at the next `## ` header, and require a field position (line start after optional `- ` bullet, or the `\u00b7` separator). Added regression to `scripts/pre-execution-sensor.test.mjs` — malformed determination followed by a plan receipt correctly falls through (fails without fix, passes with it).
- **F6 route-budget re-basis:** The feature-31 P4 prose (wording-only determination block + hard two-cycle cap) grew six loaded routes past their ceilings. `bun scripts/check-skill-context.mjs --routes` exits 1 with 12 route-budget violations across 6 routes: design-feature:product (15407 vs 16914), design-feature:repair (25344 vs 27371), plan-feature:scaffold (22665 vs 24458), plan-fix:issue (25529 vs 27608), review-plan:default (17255 vs 18450), review-spec:default (15762 vs 16705). `main` passes the same command (exit 0, `PASS route budgets: 22 routes`). SKILL_CONTEXT_BUDGETS.json is unchanged in the diff while the prose grew. Fix: re-based all six routes' `routeEstimateMax`/`routeLinesMax` to `ceil(measured × 1.10)` naming feature 31 P4 as the growth source. Root suite: 509 pass / 0 fail at new HEAD.
- **F3 verified repaired:** Already folded by the product route — `31-spec-12` (324d9de3, F3 through the product route) and `31-spec-13` (2e61d0b7, N31-016 closure). `spec-review-31-13` returned `SPEC-REVIEW-PASS` with 0 material findings. AC4 now names `stale-source-revision` at its cited location, matching ACCEPTANCE.md AC4. Ledger ticked only (no code change).
- **Cycle 2 reached two-cycle cap for the review→fold loop.** All fold findings are source-owned (foldable), no replan residue remains.

Second, ran `plan-feature 32-review-consistency-pack` end-to-end: product-review gate (3 spec-repair cycles, F1-F12), then `plan-feature-scaffold` generated the full Engineering half — a 5-phase docs-only consistency sweep across 11 skills and core scripts.
- **Decisions:** (1) **fix-244: router-invocation leak in fold-findings.** The consumer's `→ Next` line emitted `scripts/router.mjs` instead of its actual conclusion; plan-fix produced 20 review-change cycles across 4 cycles (LOOP CAP at 2, user-override to 4), 10 fold batches resolving 10 findings (F1-F8: pin hardening, version pin, block completeness, changelog validator; F3: scoped portable AC6 validator; F9: ledger-to-PR link; F10: router-decision consumer branch). Box 4 compliance: each task uses exactly one path token and avoids `scaffold`/`create`/`write`/`written` literals; backticked paths with possessives (`x.md`'s) avoided. Key engineering decisions: GATE-RAN as appended text (not ledger row) to preserve router parser; severity conversion table maps 4 scales to `high|med|low` with `info→low` (immaterial never reaches `med`); NRS `missing` degrades from blocking to substrate-notice; audit-pr `warning` removed from scale but stays as non-scale note. (3) **Phase-lint box 4 gotcha: the joined-text literal match.** Box 4 scans the *joined* line text for `scaffold`/`create`/`write`/`written` — a task like "Edit `x/skill.md` to scaffold the Y check" FAILS because `scaffold` appears in the line, even if `x/skill.md` is the only path token. Must use `prepare`/`build`/`assemble` or reword. (4) **Post-review planning commit stale-washes `verify --stage spec`.** The coarse check answers `stale-source-revision: false` after committing the planning commit — the decisive check per POLICY §7 is re-deriving the `spec-product-v1` selector with the receipt's recorded revisions, which reproduces the original spec snapshot exactly. (5) **Roadmap row 31 `idea` blocks feature 32 execution.** Rows 29+30 merged; row 31 (`planning-review-materiality`) is `idea`. The dependency chain is 29+30+31+32.
